				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = MGU_Magic_Doll_army_3_2

					remove_idea = MGU_Magic_Doll_army_3_1

				}
			}
			if = {

				}
				country_event = magicunion.1071
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = MGU_Magic_Doll_army_3
					add_idea = MGU_Magic_Doll_army_3_1
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
		#    add_manpower = -200000

				var = ubs_save
				value = -200
				tooltip = UBS_SAVE_down_200
